We present a conceptual framework for Collaborative Joint Perception and Prediction (Co-P&P) that improves motion prediction of surrounding road users, thereby enhancing situational awareness in complex and dynamic traffic environments. Building upon our preliminary study, this extended version compares the performance of different fusion strategies and establishes baseline performance for a modular design of perception and prediction. Experimental results show that prediction-level fusion leads to a decline in overall system performance compared to detection-level or tracking-level fusion. We further implement a minimal end-to-end Co-P&P prototype that couples collaborative point-cloud sharing via the RENO neural codec with joint detection-forecasting via FutureDet, showing that collaboration improves forecasting accuracy while neural compression preserves this benefit at roughly 34x lower communication bandwidth.

</details>


### [231] [PressureMesh: 3D Human Mesh Estimation from Multi-Device Pressure Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09550)
*Changhai Ma,Ziyu Wu,Yunkang Zhang,Fangting Xie,Mengting Niu,Heyu Ding,Quan Wan,Jiayue Yuan,Boyan Liu,Yi Ke,Xiaohui Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MDP-Net，一个端到端网络，能够从多个设备的时序压力数据中直接估计人体网格，突破了传统单设备压力感知在监测范围上的限制。作者还构建了包含2D/3D关节和人体网格等多种姿态标签的高质量多设备时序压力数据集MDP，实验表明MDP-Net在该数据集上达到12.6cm的关节位置误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体姿态监测在康复评估和人机交互等领域至关重要，基于压力的监测方式因其隐私保护特性而被广泛采用，但现有方法局限于单一设备，导致有效监测范围受限。

Method: 提出MDP-Net端到端网络，可直接从跨设备时序压力数据中估计人体网格；引入受Mixture of Experts (MoE)框架启发的多模态融合机制，实现跨设备压力信息的有效互补与增强；并构建了包含2D/3D关节及人体网格等多类姿态标签的高质量多设备时序压力数据集MDP。

Result: 在MDP数据集上，MDP-Net实现了12.6cm的关节位置误差。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，融合多设备压力信息是日常人体姿态监测中一种有效且有前景的新方案。

Abstract: Human pose monitoring is crucial in fields such as rehabilitation assessment and human-computer interaction. Due to its privacy-preserving nature, pressure-based human pose monitoring has become a primary approach for unobtrusive sensing. However, existing methods are generally limited to a single device, which restricts the effective monitoring range. To address this limitation, we propose MDP-Net, an end-to-end network capable of directly estimating human meshes from temporal pressure data across multiple devices. We introduce a multimodal fusion mechanism inspired by the Mixture of Experts (MoE) framework to achieve effective complementarity and enhancement of cross-device pressure information. To support the training and evaluation of MDP-Net, we constructed MDP, a high-quality multi-device temporal pressure dataset that includes various pose labels such as 2D/3D joints and human meshes. Experimental results demonstrate that MDP-Net achieves a joint position error of 12.6 cm on the MDP dataset. These results prove that fusing multi-device pressure information is an effective and promising new solution for daily human pose monitoring.

</details>


### [232] [From Semantic Grounding to Decision Optimization: A Unified Framework for Long-Horizon UAV Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09564)
*Zeyuan Ma,Jiaxin Chen,Di Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的"语义到决策"框架，解决UAV视觉语言导航中的三个耦合问题：指令相关地标的语义接地弱、长程历史利用不足、局部陷阱导致的不稳定决策。通过指令接地语义增强模块、相关性感知动态时序聚合策略和拓扑感知决策方法，在AerialVLN和OpenFly基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN方法面临三个相互耦合的问题：(1) 指令中相关地标在视觉观测中的语义接地不足；(2) 长程历史信息利用不充分；(3) 在局部陷阱或重复探索时决策不稳定。这三个问题相互关联，需要统一框架解决。

Method: 提出统一的语义到决策框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 指令接地语义增强模块：将物体级语义和相对空间线索注入当前观测状态；(2) 相关性感知动态时序聚合策略：对完整历史缓冲进行重加权，并将少量高相关帧转换为结构化的地标提示供解码器使用；(3) 拓扑感知决策方法：结合局部最优认知与组相对策略优化，在进度、目标、语义和路径合规性四类奖励下进行训练。

Result: 在AerialVLN和OpenFly两个广泛使用的基准数据集上进行实验，所提出的方法取得了最先进的性能，验证了统一框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将语义增强、时序聚合和拓扑感知决策统一在一个框架中，有效解决了UAV视觉语言导航中的关键挑战，并在两个主流基准上证明了其优越性。

Abstract: UAV vision-language navigation (UAV-VLN) focuses on enabling an aerial agent to follow natural-language instructions in open 3D environments from egocentric visual observations. Current approaches suffer from three coupled issues: weak grounding of instruction-relevant landmarks in visual observations, insufficient exploitation of long-horizon history, and unstable decisions under local traps or repeated exploration. To address these issues, we propose a unified semantic-to-decision framework. First, we present an instruction-grounded semantic enhancement module that injects object-level semantics and relative spatial cues into the current observation state. Subsequently, we develop a relevance-aware dynamic temporal aggregation strategy that reweights the full history buffer while converting a few high-relevance frames into structured landmark prompts for the decoder. Finally, we devise a topology-aware decision method that combines local-optimum cognition with group-relative policy optimization under progress, goal, semantic, and path-compliance rewards. Experiments on the widely used AerialVLN and OpenFly benchmarks clearly demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [233] [VideoVIBE: A Video-Grounded Diagnostic Benchmark for One-Shot Interactive Website Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09573)
*Jiajun Xu,Yanghao Zhou,Jingyun Liao,Yu Bai,Jinxing Zhou,Chengliang Liu,Changsen Yuan,Bo Wang,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoVIBE基准与V2Lens多智能体系统，通过视频为基础对"vibe coding"生成的Web应用进行细粒度质量诊断，弥补了现有评估仅关注孤立工件或最终任务结果的不足。基准包含约1.7K个视频问答实例、6,338个已验证失败案例，涵盖语义逻辑、视觉运动、结构时序和功能四类失败。V2Lens采用免训练的、基于证据的多智能体框架，通过定向视觉与源代码验证来挑战并选择性修正初始视频诊断，在Gemini-2.5-Flash（最强单模型，64.54分）基础上提升7.18分至71.72，展示了视频导向评估的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对自然语言驱动"vibe coding"生成的Web应用的评估方法存在不足：要么只对孤立工件打分，要么只看最终任务结果，无法提供关于失败类型及成因的细粒度证据，导致诊断信息匮乏。

Method: 1) 构建VideoVIBE基准：将人工操作的网页录制视频转化为细粒度诊断任务，包含从6,338个已验证失败中提取的约1.7K视频QA实例，覆盖语义逻辑、视觉运动、结构时序和功能四类失败，以视频呈现与行为为主要依据、源代码为补充上下文；2) 提出V2Lens系统：一个免训练的、基于证据的多智能体框架，通过针对性视觉验证和源代码验证来挑战并选择性修正初始视频诊断结果。

Result: 在13个闭源和开源视频多模态大模型（Video MLLMs）上的评估中，Gemini-2.5-Flash是最强的单模型，得分64.54；而V2Lens达到71.72分，相比最佳单模型提升了7.18分。

Conclusion: 视频导向的评估能够超越孤立工件和聚合结果，提供行为上忠实、诊断信息丰富的生成应用质量评估。V2Lens证明了通过证据验证多智能体协作可以显著增强视频MLLMs的诊断能力。

Abstract: Natural-language-driven "vibe coding" enables the one-shot generation of visually rich and interactive web applications, yet reliable assessment of their quality has not kept pace. Existing evaluations often score isolated artifacts or final task outcomes, offering limited evidence about which failures occur and why. We introduce VideoVIBE, a video-grounded benchmark that transforms human-operated webpage recordings into fine-grained diagnostic tasks. It contains approximately 1.7K diagnostic Video QA instances derived from 6,338 verified failures across generated webpages, spanning semantic-logical, visual-motion, structural-temporal, and functional failures. Diagnoses are grounded primarily in recorded presentation and behavior, with webpage source code used as complementary context. We further propose V2Lens, a training-free, evidence-grounded multi-agent system that challenges and selectively refines initial video-based diagnoses through targeted visual and source-code verification. Across thirteen closed-source and open-weight Video MLLMs, Gemini-2.5-Flash is the strongest standalone model with a score of 64.54, while V2Lens reaches 71.72, an improvement of 7.18 points. Together, our results show that video-grounded evaluation can move beyond isolated artifacts and aggregate outcomes toward a behaviorally faithful and diagnostically informative account of generated application quality.

</details>


### [234] [MSP-Net: Manifold-Guided Spectral Prompt Network for Hyperspectral Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09575)
*Juliu Li,Hanlin Qin,Shuowen Yang,Jingjing Li,Yuedong Tan,Shuai Yuan,Huixin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MSP-Net（流形引导的频谱提示网络），用于高光谱目标跟踪。该网络通过图驱动的流形路由重建波段关系并形成自适应频谱分组，结合分组频谱统计与模板外观构建目标相关的动态条件提示，并随跟踪过程实时演化以适应目标外观和场景变化。在HOT2020和HOT2023数据集上，AUC超过0.80、Precision超过0.96，展现出在异构传感器和目标形变等复杂场景下的卓越鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱目标跟踪方法将高光谱图像简单视为RGB图像的多通道扩展，在固定波段顺序下进行特征融合，导致模型依赖特定传感器配置、忽视波段间的流形关系，难以泛化到异构传感器。同时，波段的判别性贡献会随目标属性和场景变化而动态改变，静态融合策略的表达能力受限。因此需要一种能够自适应建模波段关系并动态调整融合策略的网络。

Method: MSP-Net首先通过图驱动的流形路由重建波段关系并形成自适应频谱分组；然后联合集成分组频谱统计信息与模板外观信息，构建目标相关的动态条件提示，以增强目标特征并抑制背景干扰；随着跟踪进程推进，基于中间目标表征持续演化频谱条件，使提示实时适应外观和场景变化；同时利用可靠的历史状态约束目标定位和尺度波动，提升跨传感器跟踪的时间稳定性。

Result: 在HOT2020和HOT2023数据集上，MSP-Net取得AUC超过0.80、Precision超过0.96的成绩，在异构传感器、目标形变和复杂背景条件下展现出出色的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MSP-Net通过流形引导的自适应频谱分组与动态条件提示机制，有效解决了现有方法依赖固定传感器配置和静态融合策略的局限，显著提升了高光谱目标跟踪在跨传感器场景下的泛化能力和时间稳定性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral object tracking leverages abundant spectral information to provide unique advantages for target discrimination in complex scenes. However, existing methods typically treat hyperspectral images as multi-channel extensions of RGB images, performing feature fusion in fixed band order. This approach leads to models dependent on specific sensor configurations while neglecting manifold relationships between bands, making generalization to heterogeneous sensors difficult. Moreover, the discriminative contribution of bands dynamically changes with target attributes and scene variations, further limiting the representational capacity of static fusion strategies. To address this, we propose the Manifold-Guided Spectral Prompt Network (MSP-Net). This network first reconstructs band relationships and forms adaptive spectral grouping through graph-driven manifold routing, then jointly integrates grouped spectral statistics with template appearance to construct target-related dynamic conditional prompts, enhancing target features while suppressing background interference. Furthermore, as tracking progresses, spectral conditions continuously evolve based on intermediate target representations, enabling target prompts to adapt in real-time to appearance and scene changes. Meanwhile, reliable historical states are used to constrain target localization and scale fluctuations, significantly improving temporal stability in cross-sensor tracking. Experiments on HOT2020 and HOT2023 demonstrate that MSP-Net achieves AUC and Precision exceeding 0.80 and 0.96, respectively, exhibiting exceptional robustness under heterogeneous sensors, target deformation, and complex background conditions. The code will be released at https://github.com/GGML668897/MSP-Net.

</details>


### [235] [You Only Flow Once: Calibrated and Real-Time Radar Pose Estimation with Multi-Hypothesis Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09579)
*Jonas Leo Mueller,Sebastian Hoefler,Dario Zanca,Naga Venkata Sai Jitin Jami,Thomas Altstidl,Bjoern M. Eskofier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MH-NFPG，一种基于条件归一化流的雷达点云多人姿态分布建模方法，通过单次前向并行采样实现多假设姿态生成，在三个基准上相比扩散模型将校准误差降低最多85%，推理速度提升20倍以上，同时保持或提升姿态精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达点云稀疏且含噪，同一观测可能对应多种合理的人体姿态，确定性姿态估计本质上是病态问题（ill-posed）。现有雷达姿态估计方法均为确定性方法，将多模态分布坍缩为单一估计；基于扩散模型的方法虽能建模多假设分布，但需要逐样本的昂贵序列去噪过程，且缺乏校准的不确定性估计。

Method: 提出Multi-Hypothesis Normalizing Flow Pose Generator (MH-NFPG)。具体而言：(1) 采用时空Transformer骨干网络提取雷达点云特征；(2) 结合条件归一化流（conditional normalizing flow），将Laplace基分布变换为表达力强的后验分布；(3) 通过单次前向传播并行生成所有姿态假设，避免扩散模型所需的逐步去噪。

Result: 在三个雷达基准（MM-Fi、mmRadPose、mRI）上：(1) 校准性能全面超越扩散模型；(2) 姿态精度在两个基准上提升，在第三个上持平；(3) 推理速度比扩散模型快20倍以上；(4) 校准误差最多降低85%；(5) 扩散模型在校准上显著退化，而流方法即使在跨环境设置下也保持可靠覆盖率。

Conclusion: 归一化流可作为扩散模型在实时、不确定性感知雷达姿态估计中的实用替代方案，兼顾多假设建模能力、计算效率和校准质量。代码将公开。

Abstract: Sparse and noisy millimeter-wave radar point cloud observations often correspond to multiple plausible human poses, making deterministic pose estimation fundamentally ill-posed. Yet existing radar methods remain deterministic, collapsing this ambiguity into a single estimate. Diffusion-based alternatives can model multi-hypothesis distributions but require costly sequential denoising for each distribution sample and lack calibrated uncertainty. We propose Multi-Hypothesis Normalizing Flow Pose Generator (MH-NFPG), which models pose distributions from radar point clouds using a conditional normalizing flow. Specifically, we combine a spatiotemporal transformer backbone with a normalizing flow that transforms a Laplace base distribution into an expressive posterior, generated in parallel through a single forward pass. Leveraging this efficiency, we outperform diffusion-based alternatives in calibration across three radar benchmarks (MM-Fi, mmRadPose, mRI), improve pose accuracy on two, and match it on the third, while achieving over 20x faster inference for applications and reducing calibration error by up to 85%. We find that calibration degrades substantially for diffusion models, whereas our flow-based approach maintains reliable coverage, also in cross-environment settings. These results demonstrate normalizing flows as a practical alternative to diffusion models for real-time, uncertainty-aware radar pose estimation. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [236] [GenTrack3: Hybrid Stochastic-Deterministic Online Multi-Object Tracking with Cluster-Aware Association](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09581)
*Toan Van Nguyen,Rasmus G. K. Christiansen,Dirk Kraft,Leon Bodenhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在线多目标跟踪（MOT）框架，融合确定性（detection-based数据关联）与随机性（粒子滤波）原理，以在不确定性条件下实现稳健跟踪。文章引入了一种新的轨迹-检测匹配方法以增强可扩展性并支持群体跟踪，同时通过轨迹（tracklet）携带标识符、状态、速度、轨迹惩罚与轨迹年龄等信息，并使用随机粒子集计算匹配代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性跟踪方法（如基于检测的跟踪与数据关联）虽计算高效、结果可复现，但高度依赖运动模型且对噪声检测敏感，容易产生关联错误；随机方法虽能显式建模不确定性并处理复杂非线性动力学，却面临计算开销大和随机采样导致的变异性问题。因此需要在两类方法之间寻求融合，以实现鲁棒的在线跟踪。

Method: 提出一个将确定性与随机性原理相结合的在线MOT框架。具体包括：1）一种新颖的轨迹到检测的匹配方法，通过引入可扩展的数据关联方案，在目标数量增长时保持性能并支持组级（group）跟踪；2）使用包含目标标识符、状态、速度、轨迹惩罚和轨迹年龄的tracklet作为跟踪推断机制的基础数据结构；3）每个目标关联一个随机粒子集，用于计算与检测之间的匹配代价，从而将随机建模引入关联过程。

Result: 论文提供了所提方法及基线跟踪器的参考实现，代码开源在GitHub上（https://github.com/SDU-VelKoTek/GenTrack3），便于复现和对比。文中通过该方法在不确定性条件下的跟踪表现展示了集成确定性与随机性策略的有效性，特别是在可扩展性和群体跟踪方面的改进。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了将确定性关联与随机粒子建模集成于统一在线MOT框架的可行性，为在高噪声、动态复杂的场景中实现兼具效率、鲁棒性与可扩展性的多目标跟踪提供了新思路。开源的参考实现有助于后续研究的发展与验证。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) involves maintaining consistent target identities as objects dynamically enter and leave a scene. Deterministic approaches, such as tracking-by-detection with data association, produce reproducible results and are computationally efficient, but they rely heavily on motion models and are sensitive to noisy detections that can lead to association errors. In contrast, stochastic methods explicitly model uncertainty and can better handle complex non-linear dynamics, albeit at the cost of increased computational complexity and variability arising from random sampling. This paper presents an online MOT framework that integrates deterministic and stochastic principles to achieve robust tracking under uncertainty. Furthermore, a novel track-to-detection matching approach is introduced to enhance scalability with increasing target numbers while supporting group tracking. The tracking inference mechanism employs a tracklet that includes identifiers, states, velocities, track penalties and track ages of targets, supporting a systematic tracking pipeline. Each target is associated with a stochastic particle set to compute the matching cost to detections. Reference implementations of the proposed approach and baseline trackers can be found on GitHub: https://github.com/SDU-VelKoTek/GenTrack3.

</details>


### [237] [TeaMatch: Teachable Cross-Modal Representation Learning for 2D-3D Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09590)
*Chongjian Wang,Junjie Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TeaMatch是一个新颖的2D-3D跨模态匹配框架，引入"可教学性"（teachability）作为表征学习准则，通过构建模拟常见失败模式的弱学生模型，让教师模型优化学生在下游任务中的可恢复性，从而提升匹配鲁棒性，无需额外推理成本即可集成到现有coarse-to-fine匹配管线中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测无关（detection-free）的2D-3D匹配方法主要在单一模型内优化匹配，在噪声输入、低重叠和结构模糊等挑战性条件下难以保持可靠的对应关系，缺乏跨模态表征的结构一致性和鲁棒性考量。

Method: 提出可教学性（teachability）作为跨模态表征学习准则，即表征在退化输入下能被弱学习者有效恢复的能力。构建一组任务特定的弱学生模型模拟常见失败模式，让学生在训练集上模仿教师，并在独立的元划分（meta split）上评估其可恢复性；教师通过对应级别和几何感知约束进行优化，以提升学生的可靠对应恢复能力。

Result: TeaMatch可无缝集成到现有coarse-to-fine匹配管线中且不增加推理成本，在多个具有挑战性的2D-3D匹配基准上实现了最先进（state-of-the-art）的匹配鲁棒性和性能。

Conclusion: 通过"可教学性"这一新准则，TeaMatch从跨模态表征的可恢复性角度提升了2D-3D匹配的鲁棒性，验证了元学习式师生框架在匹配任务中的有效性，且不牺牲推理效率。

Abstract: Learning reliable correspondences between images and point clouds is fundamental for 2D-3D matching. Despite recent progress in detection-free methods, existing approaches primarily optimize matching within a single model and often struggle to maintain reliable correspondences under challenging conditions such as noisy inputs, low overlap, and ambiguous structures. In this work, we propose TeaMatch, a novel framework that introduces teachability as a criterion for cross-modal representation learning. We define teachability as the ability of a representation to be effectively recovered by weak learners under degraded inputs, reflecting its structural consistency and robustness. To this end, we construct a set of task-specific weak students that simulate common failure modes and train them to imitate the teacher on a training split while evaluating their recoverability on a disjoint meta split. The teacher is then optimized to improve the students' ability to recover reliable correspondences, guided by correspondence-level and geometry-aware constraints. Our framework can be seamlessly integrated into existing coarse-to-fine matching pipelines without additional inference cost. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TeaMatch improves matching robustness and achieves state-of-the-art performance on challenging 2D-3D matching benchmarks.

</details>


### [238] [Illusion or Integrity? Geometrical Consistency Metric for AIGC Video Quality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09594)
*Yifei Xue,Yuanchen Fei,Hao Zhang,Chenzhi Nie,Tie ji,Yizhen Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoCon-Bench，一个基于物理规律遵从度来评估AI生成视频质量的基准，通过定量测量跨帧几何一致性（同形矩阵/基础矩阵拟合、内点比率和几何误差）作为视频是否符合真实物理规则的代理指标，并发布了包含6个运动类别、20个场景的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频质量评估方法主要关注视觉和谐性、视频-文本一致性以及领域特定对齐，但缺乏对物理规律遵从度的定量度量指标，无法有效评估生成视频是否符合真实世界的物理规则。

Method: GeoCon-Bench通过平移估计捕捉全局运动，利用背景对应点拟合同形矩阵或基础矩阵模型，并报告内点比率和几何误差作为互补指标，以此来定量衡量生成视频的几何一致性，作为物理规律遵从度的代理。

Result: 在多种最先进的AIGC模型上进行的实验表明，GeoCon-Bench作为视频质量评估指标具有可靠性。发布了包含20个场景、6个运动类别的评估数据集。

Conclusion: GeoCon-Bench为AIGC视频质量评估提供了从物理规律遵从角度出发的新视角和定量指标，弥补了现有VQA方法在这方面的空白，可作为可靠的视频质量评估指标。

Abstract: Recently, AI-driven video generation has attracted considerable attention. This surge increases the demand for reliable video quality assessment (VQA) metrics to evaluate AI-generated content (AIGC) videos and guide model optimization. Existing studies assess video quality through visual harmony, video-text consistency, and domain-specific alignment, yet lack quantitative metrics for measuring fidelity to physical laws. To address this limitation, we present a novel benchmark that evaluates the quality of AIGC videos based on their compliance with physical principles by quantitatively measuring geometric consistency across frames extracted from generated sequences. This serves as a proxy for estimating the extent to which generated videos conform to real-world physical rules. Specifically, GeoCon-Bench captures global motion through translation estimation, fits homography or fundamental matrix models using background correspondences, and reports complementary metrics, including inlier ratio and geometric error. We also release a dataset containing 20 scenes across six motion categories. Experiments on state-of-the-art AIGC models demonstrate the reliability of GeoCon-Bench as a video quality assessment metric.

</details>


### [239] [ResemBrick: Brick Reconstruction from Photographs with Perceptual Fidelity and Buildability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09597)
*Xilun Chen,Hanwen Wan,Yusong Zhao,Zexin Lin,Ruixiang Liao,Xiaoqiang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ResemBrick提出一种从少量照片生成可手工搭建的彩色砖块模型的系统，将离散化（体素选择）与装配（可构建性）作为紧密耦合的阶段统一优化。它通过预算约束下的占用补全网络在单次前馈中决定填充哪些表面体素，并采用支持感知与前瞻感知的贪心放置及确定性修复算法保证零漂浮、零不稳定砖块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在粗粒度体素格上，视觉相似性与结构稳定性相互制约，但现有砖块构建流程只处理其中一侧，且将体素化视为固定预处理而非可优化变量。作者旨在证明离散化与装配应被当作紧耦合的阶段。

Method: 方法包含两大核心组件：(1) 预算化占用补全——将离散化重定义为分配问题，给定目标占用量后，单个分辨率条件网络在单次前馈中决定填哪些表面体素，一套权重覆盖13个分辨率；(2) 构造即可构建——结合支持感知和前瞻感知的贪心放置，配以确定性、可证明终止的修复算法，锚定所有漂浮组件。

Result: 在匹配预算下，ResemBrick在感知保真度上超越现有体素选择方法，并在未筛选的留出对象上唯一实现零漂浮、零不稳定砖块；作为完整流水线，其在既有砖块构建系统中获得最佳感知保真度。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，应把离散化与装配视为紧密耦合而非相互独立的阶段，这一思路为在硬物理装配约束与离散预算限制下生成3D内容提供了新范式。

Abstract: Producing a hand-buildable, colored brick model of a 3D object from a few casual photographs is a clean testbed for a broader challenge: generating 3D content that meets hard physical-assembly constraints under a discrete, budget-limited voxel grid. On a coarse lattice, visual resemblance and structural stability pull against each other, yet prior brick pipelines address only one side and treat voxelization as fixed preprocessing rather than a variable to optimize. We present ResemBrick, which couples the two. Budgeted occupancy completion reframes discretization as allocation: given a target occupied-voxel count, a single resolution-conditioned network decides in one feed-forward pass which surface voxels to fill for best appearance, one weight set spanning 13 resolutions. Buildability by construction then combines support- and look-ahead-aware greedy placement with a deterministic, provably terminating repair that grounds every floating component. Under a matched budget, ResemBrick surpasses existing voxel selectors in perceptual fidelity while uniquely reaching zero floating and zero unstable bricks on unfiltered held-out objects; as a complete pipeline, it attains the best perceptual fidelity among prior brick-construction systems. Our results point to treating discretization and assembly as tightly coupled stages rather than independent ones.

</details>


### [240] [Marrying Optimal Transport and ODEs for Unified Continuous-Time 4D Reconstruction and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09613)
*Liying Yang,Hao Mo,Jialun Liu,Chen Liu,Xinxing Yu,Chenhao Guan,Hui Ma,Xiao Cao,Ajian Liu,Yanyan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Uni4R是一个统一框架，通过最优传输（OT）和常微分方程（ODE）学习连续速度场，同时提升4D重建和点跟踪任务的性能，实现任意时间戳下的运动建模与SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统一4D重建和点跟踪方法通常依赖启发式插值或仅在整数时间戳预测，缺乏运动学一致性，无法对任意时间戳的动力学进行建模。

Method: 提出Uni4R框架，包含：(1) Flow Matching Guided Decoder（FMGD），通过全局速度分支提取锚点特征，利用Flow Matching理论在锚点特征流形上构造由OT定义的概率路径，生成FM引导的速度特征；(2) 点重建分支提供几何特征；(3) 局部速度预测模块联合上述特征与时间嵌入解码任意时间戳的速度；(4) 积分一致性训练策略，使用ODE求解器积分速度恢复目标点图，实现端到端监督。

Result: 实验表明Uni4R在4D重建和点跟踪任务上均达到SOTA性能，并在新提出的运动学感知基准（continuous time）上取得SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过将连续速度场作为运动学先验，统一4D重建与点跟踪任务，两者互相受益；积分一致性训练策略解决了分数帧缺乏高质量真实速度标签的问题。

Abstract: Existing unified 4D reconstruction and point tracking approaches typically rely on heuristic interpolations or just predict at integer timestamps, lacking kinematic coherence and failing to model dynamics at any arbitrary timestamp. In this paper, we propose Uni4R, a framework that unifies these tasks by learning continuous velocity fields through the synergy of Optimal Transport (OT) and Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE). Importantly, this continuous velocity field acts as a kinematic prior that mutually benefits both 4D reconstruction and point tracking. Specifically, we propose the Flow Matching Guided Decoder (FMGD). A global velocity branch first extracts anchor features that capture the global dynamic state of the sequence. Then, FMGD leverages Flow Matching (FM) theory to formulate a probability path defined by OT on the anchor feature manifold, instantiating it as FM-guided velocity features for velocity prediction. This establishes a robust kinematic inductive bias. Meanwhile, a point reconstruction branch provides geometric features. The local velocity prediction module then joint above features and time embeddings, to decode velocities at arbitrary timestamps. To overcome the absence of high-quality ground-truth velocities in fractional frames, we propose an integral-consistency training strategy. This strategy uses an ODE solver to integrate velocities to recover target pointmaps, enabling the model to be supervised end-to-end directly from integer timestamps. Experimental results demonstrate that Uni4R achieves SOTA performance in both 4D reconstruction and point tracking, and achieves SOTA in our new kinematics-aware benchmark at continuous time.

</details>


### [241] [LoRA-based Adaptation Alone Is Not Enough: Understanding the Limits of Foundation Models for Face Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09633)
*Peter Lorenz,Anjith George,Marcel Sébastien*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了32种不同架构和训练过程的基础模型（FMs）在人脸演示攻击检测（PAD）中的表现，发现零样本提示的检测性能接近随机水平，而LoRA低秩适配（少于1%可训练参数）在多数情况下可实现低于2%的集内ACER，但跨数据集性能显著下降，表明预训练表示和适配数据集对跨数据集泛化的贡献大于轻量适配策略本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PAD方法在跨数据集评估下性能严重退化（从近乎完美降至接近随机），而当前基于基础模型的PAD系统仅聚焦于CLIP架构，忽视了其他架构和训练过程的FM。因此需要系统性评估多种FM在PAD任务中的适用性。

Method: 系统评估32种基础模型，涵盖不同架构和训练过程；在MCIO基准（MSU-MFSD、CASIA-FASD、Replay-Attack、OULU-NPU）上进行零样本提示测试和LoRA低秩适配（少于1%可训练权重）下的视觉编码器微调，并进行集内与跨数据集评估。

Result: 零样本提示在各类模型族和规模上的性能接近随机水平；LoRA适配后多数情况下集内ACER低于2%，但跨数据集ACER显著升高。LoRA主要是在数据集内部优化决策边界。

Conclusion: LoRA等轻量适配策略对跨数据集泛化的贡献有限，预训练表示的质量和适配数据集的选择对跨数据集泛化影响更大，提示PAD领域需要重新思考适配策略与数据选择的重要性。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection (PAD) aims to reliably detect a wide range of presentation attacks. While PAD methods achieve strong performance within individual datasets, their performance degrades under cross-dataset evaluation. Variations in sensors or lighting conditions can reduce the effectiveness of detectors from near-perfect to nearly random. Foundation models (FMs) have emerged as a promising alternative because typical PAD datasets, such as the MCIO benchmarks (MSU-MFSD, CASIA-FASD, Replay-Attack, and OULU-NPU), are small relative to the scale used for web-based pretraining. However, existing PAD systems primarily focus on CLIP-based foundation models, while overlooking other FMs with different architectures and training procedures. This study addresses this question by systematically evaluating 32 FMs. Zero-shot prompting achieves performance near chance across model families and scales. The vision encoders, when low-rankadapted (LoRA) with fewer than 1% trainable weights, achieve below 2% intra-dataset ACER in most cases, while cross-dataset ACER is substantially higher. LoRA primarily refines the decision boundary within a dataset, suggesting that pretrained representations and the adaptation dataset play a larger role in cross-dataset generalization than the evaluated lightweight adaptation strategy.

</details>


### [242] [NeuroRefiner: Morphology-Aware Multi-Agent Refinement for 3D Fluorescence Microscopy Neuron Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09636)
*Haiyang Yan,Jinyue Guo,Yanchao Zhang,Bingqing Wang,Zhenchen Li,Jing Liu,Jiazheng Liu,Linlin Li,Hua Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuroRefiner是一个多智能体系统，通过模拟人类专家"全局观察+局部编辑"的工作流程，结合三个协作智能体（拓扑错误诊断、校正指令生成、细化质量验证）和基于3D U-Net的TopoRefineNet工具（利用跨模态特征融合生成精化掩膜），在荧光显微镜3D神经元分割中同时保留局部细节和全局拓扑结构。在BigNeuron、CWMBS和ZBFWB三个数据集上超越现有最先进方法，在ZBFWB上F1分数提升3.02%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D神经元分割方法在荧光显微镜图像中难以同时保留神经元的局部细节和全局拓扑结构，导致分割结果碎片化。稀疏且细长的神经元形态对传统分割方法构成重大挑战，亟需一种能够兼顾局部精度与全局拓扑一致性的新方法。

Method: 提出NeuroRefiner多智能体系统，将人类专家的迭代式"全局观察-局部编辑"工作流形式化。系统包含三个协作智能体：诊断拓扑错误、生成校正指令、验证细化质量。配套提出TopoRefineNet，一种基于3D U-Net的工具，利用跨模态特征融合生成精化掩膜，支撑智能体指令引导的分割细化。通过多轮智能体推理和体素级编辑实现分割优化。

Result: 在BigNeuron、CWMBS和ZBFWB三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuroRefiner优于现有的最先进方法，尤其在具有挑战性的ZBFWB数据集上F1分数提升了3.02%。

Conclusion: NeuroRefiner通过多轮智能体推理和体素级编辑，在提升分割拓扑精度的同时增强了结果的可解释性，为荧光显微镜下的3D神经元分割提供了一种新的范式，验证了将人类专家工作流智能体化用于精细分割任务的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate 3D neuron segmentation in fluorescence microscopy is critical for neuroscience. However, the sparse and elongated morphology of neurons poses significant challenges to existing segmentation methods. These methods struggle to preserve both local details and global topology, leading to fragmented results. To address this, we propose NeuroRefiner, a multi-agent system that formalizes the human expert workflow involving iterative global observation and local editing. Specifically, NeuroRefiner comprises three collaborative agents dedicated to diagnosing topological errors, generating correction instructions, and validating refinement quality. To facilitate agent instruction-guided segmentation refinement, we propose TopoRefineNet, a dedicated 3D U-Net-based tool that leverages cross-modality feature fusion to generate refined masks. Through multi-round agent reasoning and voxel-level editing, NeuroRefiner produces topologically more accurate segmentations with enhanced interpretability. Experiments on the BigNeuron, CWMBS, and ZBFWB datasets demonstrate that NeuroRefiner outperforms state-of-the-art methods, notably achieving a 3.02% improvement in F1 score on the challenging ZBFWB dataset.

</details>


### [243] [DUET: A Diversity-Quality Duet of Distillation Experts for Two-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09637)
*Zian Li,Litong Gong,Borui Liao,Pengfei Liu,Xinyu Wang,Xinyuan Wei,Yifan Gao,Tiezheng Ge,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DUET框架，通过噪声层级专家分工（sCM专家负责高噪声步生成多样结构，DMD专家负责低噪声步细化外观细节），在不牺牲多样性的前提下实现高质量的两步视频生成。进一步引入RL引导的专家适配（DUET+）解决relay接口与高噪声阶段瓶颈。基于Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B骨干网络，DUET在两步生成中达到接近DMD的质量并保留约两倍于DMD的结构多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在视频生成中表现优异，但迭代采样的高成本阻碍实际部署。少步蒸馏虽降低开销，却在两种主导范式（轨迹级蒸馏sCM与分布级蒸馏DMD）之间暴露了质量-多样性权衡问题。现有方法难以同时在极端两步生成场景中兼顾两者。

Method: DUET通过噪声层级的专家组合（noise-level duet of experts）调和两种蒸馏范式：sCM专家处理高噪声步以铺设多样化结构，DMD专家处理低噪声步以精炼外观细节。两名专家独立训练并分别使用各自原生目标函数，避免了损失层面的直接组合带来的优化困难。此外，识别出relay接口和高噪声阶段为剩余瓶颈，通过RL引导的专家适配（DUET+）加以解决。

Result: 在以Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B为骨干的实验中，DUET将两步生成质量从sCM水平提升至接近DMD，同时保持DMD约两倍的结构多样性；DUET+在维持多样性优势的基础上进一步提升了整体质量。

Conclusion: 实验结果确立了噪声层级专家分工这一简单有效的范式，可用于调和两步视频生成中多样性与质量的矛盾，为极端少步视频生成提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models have enabled high-quality video generation in recent years, but the high cost of iterative sampling hinders their practical deployment. Few-step distillation alleviates this cost, yet exposes a quality--diversity trade-off between its two dominant paradigms: trajectory-level distillation (e.g., sCM) favors diversity, whereas distribution-level distillation (e.g., DMD) favors quality. Targeting extreme two-step video generation, we introduce DUET, which reconciles the two paradigms through a noise-level duet of experts: an sCM expert takes the high-noise step to lay out diverse structure, and a DMD expert takes the low-noise step to refine appearance detail. Since the two experts are trained independently with their native objectives, DUET sidesteps the optimization difficulties of loss-level combinations and delivers quality and diversity jointly rather than trading one for the other. We further identify the relay interface and the high-noise stage as the remaining bottlenecks, and address them with RL-guided expert adaptation, yielding DUET+. With the Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B backbone, DUET lifts the two-step quality of sCM close to the level of DMD while retaining nearly all of its structural diversity---about twice that of DMD---and DUET+ further improves overall quality while preserving this diversity advantage. Together, these results establish noise-level expert specialization as a simple, effective paradigm for reconciling diversity and quality in two-step video generation.

</details>


### [244] [EgoHieraLoc: A Cortically Inspired Hierarchical Segmentation-Guided Framework for Egocentric Visual Query Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09656)
*Yifei Cao,Guolong Wang,Mingliang Hou,Xiya Bu,Daming Liu,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoHieraLoc，一个统一的视觉查询定位（VQL）框架，同时支持2D和3D定位。受人类视觉层级处理机制启发，该框架包含三个关键模块：判别式解析模块（利用分割先验提取前景感知的查询表示）、查询感知模块（通过判别性相关滤波和可变形建模进行鲁棒目标定位）、区域自适应模块（将多尺度上下文反馈到局部区域以恢复精确目标边界）。此外，提出几何-语义联合置信度（GSJC），通过结合分割置信度、局部深度一致性、多视角反投影一致性和三角化基线质量来评估每个视角对3D估计的可信度。实验在VQL-2D和VQL-3D基准上均达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉查询定位（VQL）在物体边界模糊且全局上下文无法有效指导细粒度定位时面临挑战。人类视觉通过层级化处理（快速筛选前景候选、选择性关注目标、全局与局部反馈精化感知、跨视角可信度加权）来应对这种模糊性，受此启发，作者希望构建一个模拟这种层级认知机制的统一VQL框架，以解决2D和3D定位中的模糊边界和上下文指导不足的问题。

Method: 提出EgoHieraLoc统一框架，包含：(1) 判别式解析模块（Discriminative Parsing Module），利用分割先验提取前景感知的查询表示；(2) 查询感知模块（Query-Aware Module），通过判别性相关滤波与可变形建模进行鲁棒目标定位；(3) 区域自适应模块（Regional Adaptation Module），将多尺度上下文反馈到局部区域以恢复精确目标边界。针对3D定位，引入几何-语义联合置信度（GSJC），将分割置信度与局部深度一致性、多视角反投影一致性、三角化基线质量相乘耦合，仅当视角在语义和几何上均可信时才对3D估计做出贡献。

Result: 在VQL-2D和VQL-3D基准数据集上的大量实验表明，EgoHieraLoc取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 本文通过模拟人类视觉层级处理机制，成功构建了统一的VQL框架，有效解决了2D和3D视觉查询定位中物体边界模糊和全局上下文指示不足的难题，在两种定位任务上均验证了方法的先进性。

Abstract: Visual query localization (VQL) aims to retrieve and re-localize a queried object in egocentric videos, yet remains challenging when object boundaries are ambiguous and global context cannot effectively guide fine-grained localization. Human vision handles such ambiguity through a hierarchical process: it rapidly screens foreground candidates, selectively attends to the target despite distractors, refines perception via feedback between global context and local detail, and, when a single view is unreliable, integrates evidence across viewpoints according to its credibility. Inspired by these competencies, we propose \textbf{EgoHieraLoc}, a unified framework for VQL-2D and VQL-3D. A Discriminative Parsing Module first extracts foreground-aware query representations using segmentation priors; a Query-Aware Module then performs robust target localization through discriminative correlation filtering with deformable modeling; and a Regional Adaptation Module feeds multi-scale context back into local regions to recover precise object boundaries. To extend this perceptual hierarchy to 3D localization, we introduce Geometric-Semantic Joint Confidence (GSJC), which multiplicatively couples segmentation confidence with local depth consistency, multi-view back-projection consistency, and triangulation-baseline quality, so that a viewpoint contributes to the 3D estimate only when it is credible both semantically and geometrically. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on both VQL-2D and -3D benchmarks.

</details>


### [245] [CIFA: Contextual-Intersectional Fairness Auditing for Hidden Subgroup Discovery in Face Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09669)
*Nazia Aslam,Khalid Adnan Alsayed,Thomas B. Moeslund,Kamal Nasrollahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CIFA（上下文-交叉公平性审计框架），用于识别视觉模型中由人口统计属性与上下文属性（如光照、模糊度、图像质量等）交互作用产生的隐藏子群体性能退化，并在多个数据集和模型上验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉公平性评估主要依赖聚合准确率和人口统计子群体分析，但视觉模型对光照、模糊度、图像质量、面部配饰等上下文因素也高度敏感。这些因素可能与人口统计特征交互，产生隐藏子群体，导致模型在这些子群体上性能大幅下降，而聚合准确率和人口统计公平性指标却看似可接受，掩盖了实际的不公平风险。

Method: CIFA框架包含三个层次的审计：人口统计审计（demographic auditing）、上下文审计（contextual auditing）和上下文-交叉审计（contextual-intersectional auditing），随后执行最差子群体发现（worst-group discovery）以识别并排序最脆弱的属性组合。研究在性别分类任务上使用ResNet-50和ViT-B/16两种架构，在FairFace、CelebA和UTKFace三个数据集上进行评估，并采用审计-缓解-再审计（audit-mitigate-reaudit）协议评估多种现有缓解策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，聚合准确率和仅基于人口统计的评估会掩盖显著的上下文-交叉差异。此外，通过审计-缓解-再审计协议发现，虽然部分最差子群体的差异得到减少，但没有单一缓解策略能在所有数据集和架构上一致地消除这些差异。

Conclusion: 上下文-交叉审计应作为公平性评估的重要组成部分，该框架为发现、排序和重新评估面部分析系统中隐藏子群体风险提供了可复现的方法。当前缓解策略的局限性表明需要开发更强大、更一致的公平性干预方法。

Abstract: Fairness evaluation in computer vision commonly relies on aggregate accuracy and demographic subgroup analysis. However, visual models are also sensitive to contextual factors such as illumination, blur, image quality, facial accessories, and appearance attributes. These factors may interact with demographic characteristics, producing hidden subgroups in which performance degrades substantially despite strong aggregate accuracy and apparently acceptable demographic fairness. To address this, we propose the Contextual-Intersectional Fairness Auditing Framework (CIFA), a structured framework for identifying subgroup vulnerabilities arising from interactions between demographic and contextual attributes. CIFA performs demographic, contextual, and contextual-intersectional auditing, followed by worst-group discovery to identify and rank the most vulnerable attribute combinations. We evaluate CIFA on gender classification using ResNet-50 \cite{he2016deep} and ViT-B/16 \cite{dosovitskiy2020image} across FairFace \cite{Karkkainen2021}, CelebA \cite{Liu2015}, and UTKFace \cite{Zhang2017}. Our results show that aggregate accuracy and demographic-only evaluation can mask substantial contextual-intersectional disparities. We further assess several established mitigation strategies through an audit--mitigate--reaudit protocol and find that, although some worst-group disparities are reduced, no single strategy consistently eliminates them across datasets and architectures. These findings establish contextual-intersectional auditing as an important component of fairness evaluation and provide a reproducible framework for discovering, prioritizing, and reassessing hidden subgroup risks in face analysis systems.

</details>


### [246] [MPISuperRes-PnP: A Super-Resolution Zero-Shot Plug-and-Play Reconstruction Algorithm for Magnetic Particle Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09672)
*Vladyslav Gapyak,Thomas März,Andreas Weinmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能量最小化的磁性粒子成像（MPI）超分辨率重建方法，采用plug-and-play框架将预训练的深度高斯去噪器以零样本方式集成到重建过程中，无需训练数据和额外训练，在合成与真实MPI数据上均取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPI作为一种新兴医学成像模态，即使采用最先进的基于测量的重建方法，其空间网格仍然非常粗糙，因此超分辨率（SR）技术至关重要。已有SR方法要么对系统矩阵进行上采样，要么对重建结果进行插值，但尚未将SR直接融入重建任务。此外，MPI训练数据稀缺，限制了深度学习方法的应用。

Method: 将超分辨率纳入能量最小化的重建框架中，采用plug-and-play方法推导出分裂方案，其中涉及的高斯去噪子问题由预训练的深度高斯去噪器以零样本（zero-shot）方式处理。这样既利用了深度学习的好处，又避免了训练数据的需要。通过扩展参数搜索选择超参数，并在合成数据和真实数据上进行定量与定性评估。

Result: 该方法在合成数据和真实数据（MPIData: EquilibriumModelWithAnisotropy和2D-OpenMPI Data）上均适用。预训练去噪器无需训练即可使用，行为保守，未观察到幻觉伪影。参数搜索确定的超参数在真实数据重建中表现良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的SR方法是通用的，可在未来涉及不同正则化器或不同成像任务的MPI场景中应用。该方法成功地将深度学习优势与无训练需求相结合，解决了MPI领域训练数据稀缺的问题，且不会产生幻觉伪影。

Abstract: Magnetic Particle Imaging (MPI) is an emerging medical imaging modality. MPI is based on the non-linear response of magnetic nanoparticles to an applied magnetic field and avoids ionizing radiation. The measured signal is the voltage induced in receive coils by the particles' response. Reconstructing the particle concentration from the signal constitutes the imaging task. Even using state-of-the-art measurement-based reconstruction, the associated spatial grid is very coarse, hence super-resolution (SR) techniques are important. In this work, we propose an approach for SR in MPI inspired by energy minimization. Different methods have been proposed for SR in MPI, ranging from upscaling of the associated system matrix to interpolation of the reconstruction. Here we incorporate SR into the reconstruction task via an energy minimization formulation. Following the plug-and-play approach to energy minimization we derive a splitting scheme and a SR method for MPI where the arising Gaussian denoising task is treated with a pre-trained learned Gaussian denoiser in a zero-shot fashion. This way, we incorporate benefits of deep learning without training and avoid the need of training data. Further, we provide a quantitative and qualitative evaluation of the proposed method. Hyper-parameter are selected via an extended parameter search. The found parameters are applied for reconstruction on real data. We show the applicability of our method on synthetic and on real data (MPIData: EquilibriumModelWithAnisotropy and 2D-OpenMPI Data). The proposed method employs a deep-learning denoiser without training -- thus it does not require presently scarcely available MPI training data. The denoiser behaves conservatively, i.e., no hallucination artifacts were observed. The SR approach is generic such that it can be applied in future MPI contexts involving different regularizers or different imaging tasks.

</details>


### [247] [Thinking With Tools, Not With Pixels: Tool Calls as Text Scaffolds for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09682)
*Jiahao Shao,Yuanbo Yang,Yiyi Liao,Yujun Shen,Ceyuan Yang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TextCall（"调用但不返回"）方法，通过保留工具调用的结构文本（工具名、坐标、目标描述和意图）但用占位符替换返回图像，验证了Tool-Call Scaffold Hypothesis：在当前的"用图像思考"视觉-语言模型中，真正承载信号的是工具调用时发出的结构化文本，返回的图像像素实际上是冗余载体。在LoRA、全量微调和强化学习三种设置下，TextCall均匹配或超越完整方法，同时将延迟降低29-46%并消除工具执行API调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，在工具增强的视觉-语言模型中，返回的图像像素对推理增益贡献甚微。这引发了一个关键问题：如果像素不承载增益，那么真正起作用的信号是什么？作者推测是工具调用前发出的结构化文本（工具名称、坐标、目标描述和意图），并希望通过实验验证这一假设。

Method: 提出TextCall方法：保留工具调用的文本脚手架（工具名、坐标、目标描述、意图），但将返回的图像替换为文本占位符"[Image output skipped]"。通过三项研究验证假设：(i)非必要性——在LoRA、全量微调、RL三种设置下比较TextCall与完整"用图像思考"的表现；(ii)充分性——在匹配的训练查询上比较脚手架单独输入与返回图像输入的准确率；(iii)组件特异性——将脚手架分解为推理文本和空间代码，分析各自贡献。

Result: 三项研究均支持假设：(i)TextCall在LoRA、全量微调、RL下匹配或超越完整方法，且在RL下保留工具使用，避免了完整方法中看到返回图像导致模型停止调用工具直接回答的失败模式；(ii)脚手架单独输入的准确率与返回图像输入相当；(iii)推理文本和空间代码均有贡献，但主导组件因任务而异。TextCall将延迟降低29-46%，并消除了工具执行API调用。

Conclusion: 当前"用图像思考"基准中，工具调用时的结构化文本是主要信号载体，返回图像是冗余载体。该发现对视觉-语言模型的效率优化有重要意义，但作者也指出当前基准中像素"真正承载任务信息"的任务构建仍是开放方向。

Abstract: Tool-augmented vision-language models increasingly "think with images": they call crop, zoom, or code tools and reason over the returned pixels. However, recent work using blind tests, gain decompositions, and attention analyses has shown that returned images contribute little, raising the question: if pixels do not carry the gain, what does? We hypothesize that the load-bearing signal is the structured text emitted before any returned pixel arrives: tool name, coordinates, target description, and intent. This textual scaffold encodes where to look and what to find. We introduce TextCall (call-but-no-return) to test this: it keeps the scaffold but replaces returned images with the text placeholder [Image output skipped]. Three studies support the hypothesis. (i) Non-necessity of returned pixels: across LoRA, full fine-tuning, and RL, TextCall matches or exceeds full thinking-with-images; under RL it preserves tool use at the reported checkpoint, avoiding the failure mode where, under matched settings, seeing the returned image causes the model to stop calling tools and answer directly. (ii) Sufficiency of the scaffold: on matched training queries, scaffold-only input yields equivalent accuracy to returned-image input. (iii) Component specificity: decomposing the scaffold into reasoning text and spatial code shows both components contribute, with the dominant one varying by task. Together these results support the Tool-Call Scaffold Hypothesis: in current thinking-with-images distributions, the active signal is the structured text emitted at tool-call time; the returned image is a redundant carrier. TextCall preserves accuracy while reducing latency by 29-46% and eliminating tool-execution API calls. Our claims hold for current thinking-with-images benchmarks; constructing tasks where pixels are genuinely load-bearing remains an open direction.

</details>


### [248] [Diffuse the object, keep its label: curating detector training data from a few unlabeled photographs via VLM-built 3D vegetation scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09691)
*Mario Malizia,Marnix Enting,Rob Haelterman,Ken Hasselmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督的站点适应性方法：利用视觉-语言模型从少量部署站点的未标注照片生成粗略3D植被场景，将3D物体网格置入场景自动获得标注（边界框、分割掩码、遮挡信息），用适配器微调扩散模型对渲染图像重新纹理化，并通过分级掩码锁定控制扩散对目标对象的干预程度。在一个人道主义排雷基准上，该方法训练的检测器表现优于或持平于用其他站点大量真实标注图像训练的检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏在植被中的小目标标注图像稀缺，且在不同站点间泛化能力差。传统的做法是复用其他站点收集的标签，但跨站点标签复用效果不理想。因此需要一种无需人工标注、基于部署站点自身少量未标注照片合成训练数据的方法。

Method: (1) 用视觉-语言模型从单张照片生成粗糙的3D植被场景；(2) 在场景中放置3D物体网格，直接从场景几何中获取边界框、分割掩码和逐实例遮挡标注；(3) 在照片上微调轻量适配器，调节扩散生成过程对渲染图像进行再纹理化；(4) 引入分级掩码锁定（mask-lock）机制，控制扩散对目标对象像素的处理程度。

Result: 在带有毒物体的人道主义排雷基准上，用这些合成图像训练的检测器在多个随机种子下一致地达到或超过了用更大规模其他站点真实标注数据集训练的检测器。消融实验显示：目标对象轻度扩散能改善少数类召回率，完全保护像素次之，无约束扩散会破坏目标；增益对照片和裁剪预算不敏感；域内准确率不能预测跨站点性能。

Conclusion: 用少量部署站点照片通过生成式3D场景合成与扩散再纹理化，可以替代跨站点标签复用，实现无监督的站点适应性目标检测，且掩码锁定的程度是最关键的选择因素。

Abstract: Labeled images of small objects hidden in vegetation are scarce, and detectors trained on them generalize poorly across sites. Rather than reusing labels collected at another site, we synthesize labeled training images from a handful of unlabeled photographs of the deployment site itself. A vision--language model generates a coarse 3D vegetation scene from one photograph; placing 3D object meshes in the scene yields bounding boxes, segmentation masks, and per-instance occlusion directly from the scene geometry, without manual annotation. A lightweight adapter fine-tuned on the photographs conditions a diffusion pass that re-textures the renders, and a graded mask-lock sets how much diffusion may touch the object itself. In our runs this grade was the most influential curation choice: lightly diffusing the object improves minority-class recall over fully protecting its pixels, while unrestricted diffusion dissolves it. Trained on these images, a standard detector matched or exceeded its counterpart trained on a larger labeled dataset of real images from a different site, consistently across seeds on a humanitarian-demining benchmark; the comparison is thus unsupervised site adaptation from a handful of photographs against conventional cross-site label reuse. In our ablations the gains were largely insensitive to the photograph and crop budgets, and in-domain accuracy did not predict cross-site performance.

</details>


### [249] [LookAgain: Closed-Loop GUI Grounding with Visually Grounded Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09723)
*Renshan Zhang,Haoyang Meng,Yixiao He,Rui Shao,April Hua Liu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LookAgain是一个基于"预测-回看-修正"闭环机制的GUI grounding模型。它将坐标预测视为可被反思和修正的假设，通过"locate"（定位并渲染标记回看局部区域）和"confirm"（确认或拒绝假设）两个原语实现多轮预测-回看-修正流程，并用SFT+GRPO两阶段训练，在refusal-aware和通用GUI grounding基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI grounding方法在单步准确率上表现优异，但在小目标、密集控件和分布外界面上性能急剧下降。作者将其归因于一个范式性局限：现有方法都不把产生的坐标当作需要基于新视觉证据进行反思和修正的假设，这导致三个耦合问题：缺乏事后反思、视觉证据与预测脱节、迭代缩放只精化区域而非承接并修正预测。

Method: LookAgain将grounding重新定义为多轮"预测-回看-修正"的闭环过程，包含两个原语："locate"发布坐标假设，在图像上渲染标记并附加预测区域的局部patch，将下一步推理锚定到先前预测作为空间先验；"confirm"接受或拒绝假设并终止流程。训练采用两阶段：首先用构造的反思轨迹进行SFT作为冷启动，然后用终端grounding正确性作为唯一奖励的GRPO进行强化学习。

Result: 大量实验表明LookAgain在refusal-aware和通用GUI grounding基准上均一致提升性能，达到SOTA。全面的消融实验验证了框架各组成部分的有效性。

Conclusion: 将坐标预测视为可反思和修正的假设这一范式转变，通过"预测-回看-修正"闭环机制，能有效解决现有GUI grounding方法在小目标、密集控件和分布外场景下性能退化的问题，为GUI grounding提供了一个新的研究范式。

Abstract: Recent graphical user interface (GUI) grounders have significantly advanced single-shot accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet their performance degrades sharply on small targets, densely packed controls and out-of-distribution interfaces. We attribute this gap to a paradigmatic limitation shared by existing approaches: none of them treats a produced coordinate as a hypothesis to be reflected upon and revised under new visual evidence. This manifests as three coupled issues: 1) Lack of post-hoc reflection. The prediction is frozen at the moment of emission, leaving no internal mechanism to challenge or refine it. 2) Visual evidence decoupled from the prediction. The auxiliary visual evidence is gathered to support the upcoming coordinate rather than to scrutinise the one already committed to. 3) Refinement over views, not over predictions. The iterative zoom-in refines the inspected region instead of inheriting a previous coordinate as a spatial prior to be corrected. In this paper, we propose LookAgain, a closed-loop GUI grounder driven by post-prediction visual reflection. LookAgain reformulates grounding as a multi-turn predict-look-again-refine process with two primitives: "locate" posts a coordinate hypothesis, renders a marker on the image and appends a local patch of the predicted region. It anchors the next reasoning step to the previous prediction as a spatial prior; "confirm" accepts or reject the hypothesis and terminates the procedure. We train the LookAgain grounder with SFT on constructed reflective trajectories as a cold start, followed by GRPO with terminal grounding correctness as the sole reward. Extensive experiments show that LookAgain consistently improves performance on both refusal-aware and general GUI grounding benchmarks, achieving state-of-the-art results. Comprehensive ablations further verify the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [250] [World Tokens: Enhancing Embodied Policies with Training-Time World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09730)
*Qu Tang,Benhui Zhuang,Bo Yuan,Xue Yu,Longteng Guo,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出World Tokens，一种由VLM、World Adapter和动作专家组成的embodied policy架构。通过训练时的世界建模（未来视频去噪）来增强动作策略表示，同时在部署时移除世界模型分支，仅保留VLM、World Adapter和动作专家，实现高效的闭环控制。在LIBERO、SIMPLER和真实机器人R1 Pro上均取得极具竞争力的表现，且推理延迟与VLA相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型擅长高效闭环控制但缺乏对物理场景时空演化的显式建模；而世界动作模型（WAMs）虽能建模时空演化，但在控制回路中保留未来视频生成或大型视频骨干网络会显著增加推理成本。现有方法在"世界建模能力"与"高效部署"之间存在不可调和的矛盾。

Method: World Tokens架构由三部分组成：VLM负责视觉语言理解，World Adapter将VLM特征转换为固定数量的world tokens，这些tokens同时作为（1）联合微调的未来视频去噪器的条件输入，以及（2）动作专家唯一的视觉语言上下文。通过共享条件化使未来视频去噪的梯度直接塑造用于动作预测的表示；通过独占路由（exclusive routing）防止策略绕过该表示。训练时启用世界模型分支，部署时将其移除，仅保留VLM、World Adapter和动作专家，无在线视频模型推理。

Result: 使用2B骨干模型且无embodied动作预训练的情况下，在LIBERO上极具竞争力，在SIMPLER上达到最佳平均结果，在真实机器人R1 Pro上相比匹配的动作-only基线显著提升成功率，且每个动作chunk的生成延迟与VLA级别相当。

Conclusion: World Tokens通过设计性地将世界建模约束嵌入训练过程中，在保持部署时高效性（无在线视频推理、VLA级延迟）的同时，有效提升了动作策略的性能，打破了世界建模能力与推理效率之间的权衡。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are a widely adopted paradigm for embodied policies. They excel at efficient closed-loop control but do not explicitly model how physical scenes evolve as a task unfolds. Recently emerging world-action models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video world models to capture spatiotemporal evolution, yet retaining future generation or a large video backbone in the control loop substantially increases inference cost. We introduce World Tokens, an embodied policy architecture built around a World Adapter that bridges visual-language understanding, world-dynamics modeling, and action generation. It uses world modeling during training to enhance the action policy while preserving efficient deployment. Specifically, the World Adapter transforms VLM features into a fixed set of world tokens, which condition a jointly fine-tuned future-video denoiser and simultaneously serve as the action expert's sole visual-language context. This shared conditioning allows gradients from future-video denoising to directly shape the representation used for action prediction, while exclusive routing prevents the policy from bypassing that representation. At deployment, the world-model branch is removed, leaving only the VLM, World Adapter, and action expert, with no online video-model inference. With a 2B backbone and no embodied action pretraining, World Tokens is highly competitive on LIBERO, attains the best reported averages on SIMPLER, substantially improves real-world R1 Pro success over a matched action-only baseline, and generates each action chunk at VLA-level latency.

</details>


### [251] [HandSplatter: Automated Digital Goniometry from Neural Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09735)
*Emmett Chen,Neal Chen,Xiang Li,Quanzheng Li,Siyeop Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经渲染的3D手部关节定位与姿态估计新流程，结合2D特征提取与视图合成，并引入离散密度爬山算法修正投影地标，用于提高手指关节活动度（ROM）测量的准确性和临床可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部及手指疾病是肌肉骨骼残疾的主要诱因，需要精确的关节运动量化方法。当前标准工具——关节角度计（goniometer）依赖人工操作，劳动强度大且因检查者技术差异导致评估者间信度不一致；现有的数字软件方案在临床应用中精度不足。因此需要一种既高效又准确的自动化关节测量方法。

Method: 提出一个新颖的3D手部关节定位与姿态估计流程，核心包括：(1) 神经渲染（neural rendering）框架，将2D特征提取与视图合成相结合，突破传统方法在精度上的局限；(2) 引入离散密度爬山算法（discrete density hill climbing），用于在3D空间中有效修正投影地标的位置，提升关节定位的准确性。

Result: 该系统的准确性和临床可行性显著优于传统手动测量和现有软件方案，克服了手动角度计测量的低效率以及现有数字方法的精度不足问题，为客观功能评估提供了稳健工具。

Conclusion: 该神经渲染驱动的3D手部关节测量系统为临床手指ROM评估提供了一种高精度、高效率的自动化替代方案，具备良好的临床转化潜力，可用于诊断、康复监测和术后疗效评价。

Abstract: Hand and finger disorders are leading contributors to musculoskeletal disability, creating a clinical need for precise methods to quantify joint motion. Range of motion (ROM) serves as the metric for diagnosis, rehabilitation monitoring, and evaluating surgical outcomes. Currently, the goniometer is the standard tool for assessing finger flexion and extension. However, manual goniometry is labor-intensive and suffers from inconsistent inter-rater reliability due to variations in examiner technique. While digital alternatives exist, current software-based approaches often lack the necessary accuracy for clinical usage. To address these limitations, we present a novel pipeline for 3-D hand joint location and pose estimation using neural rendering. Unlike previous methods, our approach combines 2-D feature extraction with view synthesis to significantly improve accuracy and clinical viability. Furthermore, we introduce a discrete density hill climbing algorithm that facilitates the meaningful correction of projected landmarks in 3-D space. This system overcomes the inefficiencies of manual measurement and the inaccuracies of existing software, providing a robust tool for objective functional assessment.

</details>


### [252] [NTIRE 2026 Low-light Enhancement: Twilight Cowboy Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09782)
*Aleksei Khalin,Egor Ershov,Artyom Panshin,Sergey Korchagin,Georgiy Lobarev,Arseniy Terekhin,Sofiia Dorogova,Amir Shamsutdinov,Yasin Mamedov,Bakhtiyar Khalfin,Bogdan Sheludko,Emil Zilyaev,Nikola Banić,Georgy Perevozchikov,Radu Timofte,Shuai Liu,Yuqian Zhang,Lize Zhang,Yibin Huang,Chaoyu Feng,Luyang Wang,Xiaotao Wang,Dongqing Zou,Lei Lei,Tianli Liu,Dejun Hao,Chunxia Lei,Furkan Kınlı,Andrei Mironov,Alexander Dikov,Aleksei Sadokhin,Vladimir Zvorygin,Constantine Habarlak,Shuwei Yue,Egor Mirantsov,Daniil Okunev,Dmitry Arkhipov,Aleksandr Yugay,Anas M. Ali,Bilel Benjdira,Wadii Boulila,Wei Zhou,Linfeng Li,Lingdong Kong,Jiachen Tu,Guoyi Xu,Yaoxin Jiang,Jiajia Liu,Yaokun Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文回顾了NTIRE 2026低光增强挑战赛（Twilight Cowboy），该竞赛旨在将多帧未对齐的智能手机原始域低光图像融合为一张干净图像，同时解决低光条件下的视觉退化（噪声、混合光源）和手持拍摄导致的几何不一致问题。竞赛收集了585个真实场景的数据集，采用三阶段评估协议（CodaBench自动验证+私有测试集盲评），最终10支队伍超过基线，PSNR最高提升+6.49 dB，SSIM提升+0.0101，刷新了突发序列低光图像增强的最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光手机摄影面临两个关键挑战：一是视觉退化（高噪声、混合色温光照），二是多帧拍摄时手部运动导致的几何错位。现有方法通常分别处理这两个问题，缺乏统一框架。作者希望通过竞赛形式推动同时解决这两大难题的研究进展，并构建涵盖室内低光和室外夜间真实场景的大规模数据集以促进该领域发展。

Method: 竞赛通过收集585个真实场景（室内低光+室外夜间）构建数据集，任务为将原始域中一组未对齐的智能手机低光突发图像融合为单一干净图像。评估采用三阶段协议：第一阶段和第二阶段通过CodaBench平台进行自动验证，最终排名基于私有测试集的盲评（盲法评估）。

Result: 共有10支参赛队伍超过预置基线，在PSNR上最高提升达+6.49 dB，SSIM最高提升+0.0101，创造了突发序列低光图像增强的新最先进性能。

Conclusion: 竞赛结果表明，在真实世界的噪声、运动和光照变化条件下，突发序列低光图像增强技术取得了显著进展；同时，相关结果、排行榜和额外信息已公开在https://nightimaging.org，供社区进一步研究和参考。

Abstract: This paper presents a review of the NTIRE 2026 Low-light Enhancement: Twilight Cowboy Challenge. The objective of the competition was to merge a set of misaligned smartphone images in the raw domain, captured in low-light conditions, into a single, clean image. Introduced setup simultaneously addresses two problems of low-light photography: visual degradations such as high noise and mixed scene illuminants, and the geometric inconsistencies caused by hand movement during multi-frame capture. To advance research in low-light and nighttime computational photography, a challenging dataset was collected comprising 585 real-world scenes, spanning indoor low-light and outdoor nighttime conditions, for training and benchmarking participant solutions. The competition employed a three-stage evaluation protocol: automatic validation via the CodaBench platform in stages one and two, followed by blind assessment on a private test set for the final ranking. Ten teams surpassed the established baseline, achieving improvements of up to +6.49 dB in PSNR and +0.0101 in SSIM, thereby establishing new state-of-the-art performance for burst-based low-light image enhancement. These results demonstrate significant progress in handling real-world noise, motion, and illumination variability in the low-light setting. Comprehensive results, leaderboards, and additional information are publicly available at https://nightimaging.org.

</details>


### [253] [ADOPD: Reference-Privileged On-Policy Distillation for MLLM-Based Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09789)
*Jingtai He,Shiyuan Meng,Wenchao Meng,Qinmin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ADOPD是一个参考特权在策略蒸馏框架，通过匹配/不匹配参考条件下的师生对数比定义token级学习方向，并用似然间隙校准序列级权重，使查询仅输入的MLLM在不依赖推理时参考检索的情况下将参考比较能力内化到模型参数中，在MMAD基准零样本推理下达到77.31%平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在工业异常检测（IAD）中通过推理时比较查询图像与参考图像可以提升识别精度，但这依赖于额外的检索与处理开销。研究者希望探究能否将参考比较带来的收益内化到模型参数中，从而在推理时无需参考即可获得同等甚至更好的性能。

Method: 提出ADOPD（reference-privileged on-policy distillation framework）。具体而言：(1) 利用参考感知的教师模型在训练时监督查询仅输入的学生模型；(2) 教师评估学生在匹配与不匹配参考条件下生成的rollouts；(3) 匹配参考条件下的教师-学生log比定义了token级学习方向；(4) 两种参考视图之间的似然差距估计参考特定支持度，用于校准序列级权重。

Result: 在MMAD基准上零样本推理取得77.31%平均准确率，相比Qwen3-VL-4B骨干提升6.14个百分点，并超过其one-shot设置2.64个百分点。实验表明ADOPD能从参考比较中学习到细粒度的异常检查策略。

Conclusion: ADOPD验证了参考比较的收益可以通过参考特权蒸馏被内化到查询仅输入模型中，在零样本推理下超越了显式参考检索的one-shot性能，说明训练阶段的参考特权可以替代推理阶段的外部参考依赖。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection (IAD) requires identifying fine-grained deviations from normal visual patterns. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can improve recognition accuracy by comparing query images with references at inference time, but these benefits rely on additional retrieval and processing. We investigate whether the benefits of reference comparison can instead be internalized in the model parameters. Access to references during training allows a reference-aware teacher to supervise a query-only student. However, the teacher may favor plausible responses based on query cues or language priors rather than valid visual information. We propose ADOPD, a reference-privileged on-policy distillation framework. The teacher evaluates student-generated rollouts under matched and mismatched references. The matched-reference teacher-to-student log-ratio defines the token-level learning direction, specifying what the student should learn. The likelihood gap between the two reference views estimates reference-specific support and calibrates the sequence-level weight. ADOPD achieves 77.31% average accuracy on the MMAD benchmark under zero-shot inference, improving the Qwen3-VL-4B backbone by 6.14 points and outperforming its one-shot setting by 2.64 points. Experiments show that ADOPD learns a fine-grained anomaly inspection strategy from reference comparison. The project will be available at https://github.com/withTai/ADOPD.

</details>


### [254] [Modern Backbones Improve Multi-task DETR for Mammography Classification and Lesion Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09801)
*Dinh Tan Nguyen,Quang-Hien Kha,Le-Hoang Nguyen,Minh-Toan Dinh,Xuan-Huy Nguyen,Dac Phu Ho,Cao Truong Tran,Sai Ho Ling,Lan T Ho-Pham,Liem Pham,Nguyen Quoc Khanh Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在乳腺X线摄影中同时进行考试级恶性预测和候选区域定位的多任务学习框架，使用基于DETR的架构，并比较了多种现代骨干网络在两个数据集（OPTIMAM和活检确认的SGM1k）上的表现，发现ConvNeXtV2和DINOv3表现最佳，其中ConvNeXtV2在OPTIMAM上最优，DINOv3在SGM1k上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合进行考试级预测和候选区域定位可以提升AI在乳腺X线摄影中的实用性，但目前缺乏对这一多任务设置下不同骨干网络性能的系统性评估。

Method: 采用多任务DETR框架，利用共享表示同时支持图像级恶性预测和病灶定位，在OPTIMAM和活检确认的SGM1k两个数据集上进行评估，对比多种现代骨干网络与传统ResNet风格特征的性能。

Result: 现代骨干网络一致优于旧式ResNet风格特征。OPTIMAM上ConvNeXtV2达到97.96% AUC、99.89%敏感度、25.08% mAP@.5和74.38% recall@.25；SGM1k上DINOv3达到90.97% AUC、86.28%敏感度、82.00%特异度、27.04% mAP@.5和77.32% recall@.25。

Conclusion: 骨干网络质量是多任务乳腺X线摄影中效果的关键因素，ConvNeXtV2在该框架下是特别强大且匹配良好的CNN骨干网络。

Abstract: Joint exam-level prediction and candidate-region localization may improve the usefulness of AI support in mammography. We study this setting using a multi-task DETR framework, where shared representations support both image-level malignancy prediction and lesion localization, and evaluate its performance on OPTIMAM and a biopsy-confirmed SGM1k cohort. Across both datasets, modern backbones consistently outperformed older ResNet-style features, with ConvNeXtV2 and DINOv3 giving the strongest overall results, whereas MambaVision was less competitive. On OPTIMAM, ConvNeXtV2 achieved the best overall performance, reaching 97.96% AUC, 99.89% sensitivity, 25.08% mAP@.5, and 74.38% recall@.25. On SGM1k, DINOv3 gave the strongest overall results, with 90.97% AUC, 86.28% sensitivity, 82.00% specificity, 27.04% mAP@.5, and 77.32% recall@.25. These findings suggest that backbone quality is a critical factor in effective multi-task mammography, with ConvNeXtV2 emerging as a particularly strong and well-matched CNN backbone for mammography in this framework.

</details>


### [255] [MedPixel: A Unified Pixel-Language Model for Medical Reasoning and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09818)
*Haoyu Yang,Meixing Shi,Zengjie Chen,Haoran Sun,Haitao Leng,Xiaoming Shi,Yuxiang Cai,Yankai Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedPixel是一个统一的医学像素-语言模型，通过共享的语言-掩码接口桥接医学视觉语言模型（缺乏精确定位）与医学分割模型（依赖明确类别或空间提示）之间的鸿沟。作者构建了MedPLG-440K数据集（约44万像素-语言任务样本，通过临床动机的合成流程生成，无需外部LLM标注），并采用联合多任务监督微调加像素级偏好优化进行训练，在显式定位、隐式推理、空间交互、可解释性分割和医学VQA等多任务上取得强性能，并展示了良好的零样本迁移能力和对不完美空间提示的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像理解需要模型同时具备语言理解、视觉推理和像素级定位能力，但现有医学视觉语言模型缺乏精确定位能力，医学分割模型又依赖明确的类别或空间提示。根本原因是监督信号不匹配：分割数据集提供精确掩码但缺乏语言监督，医学视觉语言数据又很少将语言与密集空间标注配对。这一鸿沟限制了统一的医学像素-语言模型的构建。

Method: MedPixel采用共享的语言-掩码接口统一处理语言与像素两种模态。为解决监督信号稀缺问题，提出MedPLG-440K数据集，包含约44万组像素-语言任务样本，通过基于临床动机的合成流程构建，无需外部LLM标注。训练过程分为两个阶段：首先进行联合多任务监督微调，随后应用像素级偏好优化（Pixel-Level Preference Optimization），利用真实掩码作为离线验证器，从掩码质量中推导响应偏好，从而在无需额外人工反馈的情况下优化模型输出。

Result: MedPixel在显式定位、隐式推理、空间交互、可解释性分割和医学VQA等广泛任务上均实现了像素级预测和响应生成方面的强性能，同时展现出对外部定位基准的零样本迁移能力和对不完美空间提示的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MedPixel成功弥合了医学视觉语言模型与医学分割模型之间的鸿沟，证明了通过自合成的像素-语言数据扩展和基于掩码质量的离线偏好优化，可以在统一的框架内同时实现精确的像素级定位和高质量的语言响应生成，为医学像素级多模态智能提供了可行的范式。

Abstract: Reliable medical image understanding requires models to connect clinical language and visual reasoning with pixel-level grounding. Yet medical vision-language models often lack precise localization, whereas medical segmenters typically rely on explicit target categories or precise spatial prompts. This divide is reinforced by a supervision mismatch: segmentation datasets provide precise masks but little language supervision, whereas medical vision-language data rarely pair language with dense spatial annotations. To address this gap, we present MedPixel, a unified medical pixel-language model built around a shared language--mask interface. To provide scalable supervision, we introduce MedPLG-440K, comprising approximately 440K pixel-language task samples constructed through a clinically motivated synthesis process without external LLM annotation. MedPixel is trained with joint multi-task supervised fine-tuning followed by Pixel-Level Preference Optimization, which uses ground-truth masks as offline verifiers to derive response preferences from mask quality. MedPixel supports a broad spectrum of tasks spanning explicit grounding, implicit reasoning, spatial interaction, grounded explanation, and medical VQA. Across this task spectrum, MedPixel achieves strong performance in both pixel-level prediction and response generation, together with effective zero-shot transfer to external grounding benchmarks and robustness to imperfect spatial prompts. Code and model checkpoints will be released at https://github.com/yhy-whu/Medpixel.

</details>


### [256] [From Diagnosis to Correction: Benchmarking and Improving Real-World Table Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09842)
*Jutao Xiao,Yuan Qu,Dongsheng Ma,Fan Wu,Tianyao He,Weihong Li,Jie Yang,Yu Qiao,Bin Wang,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TableParseMap诊断基准（916张真实世界复杂表格，涵盖5种场景、9类失败类型），并揭示现有解析器在聚合基准上的高分（>93 TEDS）掩盖了真实复杂表格上的严重缺陷（最佳仅85.03 TEDS）。作者进一步提出DEC（Decompose-Enhance-Correct）智能体框架，通过通用VLM作为控制器，在不重新训练的情况下提升冻结解析器的性能，在TableParseMap上平均提升1.89 TEDS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析器在OmniDocBench等聚合基准上已达到93+的TEDS高分，但社区反馈和实际审计显示其在复杂真实世界表格上仍存在持续失败。聚合基准分数掩盖了模型在特定困难场景下的显著弱点，需要更细粒度的诊断基准来量化这一差距。

Method: 首先构建TableParseMap诊断基准（916张真实表格、5种挑战场景、9类失败类型），通过审计分析发现三类根本原因：大表格超出单次处理可靠规模、弱或模糊的视觉线索阻碍结构感知、重建表格与图像视觉不一致。据此提出DEC框架：Decompose沿结构感知边界分割大表格；Enhance暴露弱视觉证据并重新解析变换后的视图；Correct诊断并修复残余错误。VC-Gate选择性触发干预，VC-Ranker在无ground-truth HTML的推理阶段验证候选更新并支持回滚。同时通过离线条目和跨模型共识从4,556个候选中筛选出1,977个表格的Consensus-Hard Set。

Result: 最强评估解析器在TableParseMap上仅达85.03 TEDS。DEC在3个冻结解析器上平均提升1.57 TEDS；在TableParseMap上总体提升1.89，结构性错误上提升2.62，大表格上提升5.66 TEDS。

Conclusion: 聚合基准分数确实掩盖了复杂真实表格上模型的实质弱点，DEC通过视觉一致性引导的智能体框架无需重训即可显著提升冻结解析器的效果，特别是在大多表格和结构性错误上。视觉一致性作为推理时验证信号，是无需ground-truth校正表格解析的有效途径。

Abstract: Recent document parsers achieve table TEDS scores above 93 on OmniDocBench v1.6, yet community feedback and our audit reveal persistent failures on complex real-world tables. To quantify this gap, we introduce TableParseMap, a diagnostic benchmark of 916 real-world tables organized into five challenging scenarios and nine failure types. The strongest evaluated parser achieves only 85.03 TEDS, showing that aggregate benchmark scores conceal substantial weaknesses. Our analysis attributes these failures to three complementary limitations: large tables exceed the reliable processing scale of a single pass, weak or ambiguous visual cues hinder structure perception, and the reconstructed table may remain visually inconsistent with the image. We therefore propose DEC (Decompose--Enhance--Correct), a visual-consistency-guided agentic framework that improves frozen table parsers without retraining. DEC uses a general VLM as the controller: Decompose partitions large tables along structure-aware boundaries, Enhance exposes weak visual evidence and reparses transformed views, and Correct diagnoses and repairs residual errors. A Visual Consistency Gate (VC-Gate) selectively triggers intervention, while a Visual Consistency Ranker (VC-Ranker) verifies candidate updates and supports rollback without ground-truth HTML at inference time. We further derive a 1,977-table Consensus-Hard Set from 4,556 candidates through offline metrics and cross-model consensus. Across three frozen parsers, DEC improves TEDS by 1.57 points on average; on TableParseMap, gains reach 1.89 points overall, 2.62 on structural errors, and 5.66 on large tables.

</details>


### [257] [Sci-VBench: Evaluating Knowledge- and Reasoning-Intensive Video Generation in Science Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09873)
*Diandian Zhang,Tingyu Song,Lin Fu,Zheyuan Yang,Yilun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sci-VBench，一个面向科学领域的知识密集型和推理密集型视频生成基准，包含1,253个来自四个核心学科的专家标注示例，并建立了基于评分标准的评估协议，对16个前沿模型进行基准测试后发现视觉真实性与科学因果正确性之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成基准主要评估表面视觉合理性，缺乏对科学推理和知识基础合成的严格要求。随着视频生成模型快速发展，需要一个能够评估模型在科学领域知识密集型和推理密集型视频生成能力的综合基准，以推动模型从"视觉上看起来逼真"向"科学上正确"发展。

Method: 构建了包含1,253个专家标注示例的基准数据集，覆盖自然科学、医疗健康、人文社科和工程四大核心学科的60个主题。建立了基于评分标准的评估协议，引入非专家人工评估者和MLLM-as-Judge系统进行验证，并基准测试了16个前沿商业及开源模型。

Result: 在提出的评估协议下，非专家人工评估者和多模态大语言模型评判系统与专家判断具有较高一致性，支持规模化可复现评估。对16个模型的基准测试显示，自动感知质量分数在各系统间紧密聚类，但提示接地性和科学及因果正确性表现差异显著，且商业模型与开源模型之间存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，当前视频生成模型在视觉真实感上的进步尚未转化为对科学和因果动态的可靠建模能力。这揭示了未来视频生成研究需要重点关注的挑战方向，即提升模型在科学知识理解和推理方面的能力。

Abstract: We introduce Sci-VBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating knowledge- and reasoning-intensive video generation across scientific domains. It contains 1,253 expert-annotated examples spanning 60 subjects across four core disciplines: Natural Science, Healthcare, Humanities & Social Sciences, and Engineering. Each example requires models to generate temporally rich videos that demand scientific reasoning and knowledge-grounded synthesis, going beyond surface-level visual plausibility. We further establish a rubric-based evaluation protocol. Our analysis shows that, under this protocol, both non-expert human evaluators and MLLM-as-Judge systems can achieve relatively high agreement with expert judgments, supporting reproducible evaluation at scale. We benchmark 16 frontier proprietary and open-source models and find that, while automatic perceptual-quality scores cluster tightly across systems, performance on Prompt Grounding and Scientific and Causal Correctness varies substantially, with a pronounced proprietary-open-source gap. These findings show that advances in visual realism have not yet translated into reliable modeling of scientific and causal dynamics.

</details>


### [258] [Financial Numerical Prediction and Allocation as Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09880)
*Xu Ouyang,Moontae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FinATOM框架，探索用因果语言模型直接通过受约束的token生成来表示金融预测和决策，取代传统的任务特定回归头。该框架包含三步股票收益预测和动态五ETF资产配置两个任务，通过序数/排名监督、策略阶段SFT和DAPO增强的GRPO等训练方式，在2023-2025年ETF测试中实现了显著的Sharpe比率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融预测通常依赖特定任务的回归、排序或策略头，将语言模型与最终评估的数值对象分离。本文质疑这种范式，探索因果语言模型是否可以直接通过受约束的token生成来表示预测和决策，从而统一语言理解与数值预测。

Method: FinATOM提出统一的无头(head-free)接口：1)预测模型自回归生成波动率标准化的收益token，采用序数和排名监督训练，再加单epoch的token级策略阶段；2)配置模型生成归一化多头权重，SFT模仿因果均值-方差锚定策略，并用DAPO增强的GRPO优化21天实现Sharpe，同时保持锚定一致性约束；3)采用多模态输入。

Result: 在2023-2025年ETF测试中：分配策略将池化总Sharpe从1.428提升至1.529，5bp交易成本下的净Sharpe从1.394提升至1.494；多模态分配输入实现最高三期平均Sharpe 1.540，2025年优势最为明显。在FinTexTS上，SFT和策略方法分别达到73.52%/2.68和73.72%/2.69的累计收益率/Sharpe。

Conclusion: 结果表明直接语言模型token生成用于金融数值预测和决策是可行的，为统一语言模型与金融决策提供了新范式，同时需要跨更多资产、市场状态和随机种子进行更广泛的测试验证。

Abstract: Financial prediction typically relies on task-specific regression, ranking, or policy heads, separating the language model from the numerical object ultimately evaluated. We investigate whether a causal language model can instead represent forecasts and decisions directly through constrained token generation. FinATOM introduces a unified, head-free interface for three-step stock-return forecasting and dynamic five-ETF allocation. The forecasting model autoregressively emits volatility-standardized return tokens and is trained with ordinal and ranking supervision followed by a one-epoch token-level policy stage. The allocation model generates normalized long-only weights; supervised fine-tuning imitates a causal mean--variance anchor, and DAPO-augmented GRPO optimizes realized 21-day Sharpe subject to anchor consistency. In 2023--2025 ETF tests, the allocation policy improves pooled gross Sharpe from 1.428 to 1.529 and net Sharpe under a 5-bp transaction-cost model from 1.394 to 1.494. The multimodal allocation input attains the highest three-period mean Sharpe of 1.540, with its clearest advantage in 2025. On FinTexTS, the SFT and policy strategies achieve 73.52\%/2.68 and 73.72\%/2.69 cumulative-return/Sharpe, respectively. These results support the feasibility of direct language-model token generation for financial numerical prediction and decision-making, while motivating broader tests across assets, regimes, and random seeds.

</details>


### [259] [Space-Creating versus Dead Possession: An Off-Ball Possession-Quality Index for Broadcast Football](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09887)
*Seongjin Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出两层级框架来区分足球中的"无效控球"与"创造空间的控球"：第一层基于事件数据的"垃圾控球指数"（Junk-Possession Index）识别在平局或落后状态下低威胁的控球序列；第二层通过广播视频投影构建"空间创造指数"（SCI），衡量控球期间净球场控制变化，以区分空间死亡型与空间创造型控球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于事件的控球价值框架（expected threat、VAEP、on-ball value）只对持球动作定价，忽略了无球层面的关键问题：一次控球到底创造了空间，还是只是死循环？论文指出"控球率"是最常被引用但也最误导的指标——在自己的半场循环60%的控球与压制对手60%的控球完全不同。

Method: 设计两层级方案。第一层（事件侧）：构建"垃圾控球指数"，基于expected-threat网格对每次控球序列按峰值威胁增益定价，并重建实时比分排除领先保护型循环，在平局或落后状态下标记低威胁序列。第二层（视频侧）：对标记的控球窗口，将广播视频投影到球场坐标，计算"空间创造指数"（SCI）——衡量净球场控制变化（净pitch-control变化），以判断控球是夺取了空间还是被对方防线推回。

Result: 2026年FIFA世界杯103场比赛、206支球队样本中，垃圾控球标记与积分成负相关（r=-0.37），与xG差负相关（r=-0.51）。在控制球队进攻VAEP和球场倾斜后，垃圾标记仍是积分的强负向预测因子（p<0.0001，匹配聚类），而VAEP不显著——表明该指标提供超出on-ball价值模型的信息。视频层面对9场比赛35个标记窗口的31个进行判别：74%为空间非创造型，19%为弱推进型，6%为事件标记会误判为失败的空间创造型窗口。一个73%控球率但点球出局的球队出现两个非创造窗口。

Conclusion: 两层级框架能够将"创造了空间但未转化得分"与"无效控球"区分开来，这是仅依赖事件数据的on-ball价值模型无法实现的。垃圾控球指数是on-ball价值（如VAEP）的有力补充而非替代品，二者捕捉的是控球质量的不同维度。

Abstract: Ball possession is the most-cited and most-misleading number in football: 60% recycled in one's own half is not 60% spent pinning the opponent back. Existing event-based possession-value frameworks (expected threat, VAEP, on-ball value) price on-ball actions but ignore the off-ball question a sterile possession poses: did holding the ball create space, or was the circulation dead? We answer this in two layers. First, an event-side junk-possession index prices each possession sequence by its peak threat gain under an expected-threat grid and -- after reconstructing the live scoreline to exclude lead-protecting circulation -- flags low-threat sequences in tied-or-losing states. On the 2026 FIFA World Cup (103 matches, 206 team-matches) the flag correlates negatively with points (r=-0.37) and xG difference (r=-0.51, partly index-coupled). It is not a repackaging of on-ball value: with team offensive VAEP and field tilt held fixed, the junk flag stays strongly negatively associated with points (p<0.0001, also match-clustered) while VAEP is not significant -- in this same-match (descriptive) regression it adds information beyond this on-ball action-value model. Second, for a flagged window we resolve whether it was spatially dead or space-creating by projecting broadcast video to pitch coordinates and measuring a Space-Creation Index (SCI): a net pitch-control change capturing whether the possession seized space or pushed the opponent's block back. Across 31 of 35 flagged windows from nine World Cup matches (a purposive sample), 74% are spatially non-space-creating, 19% weak progression, and 6% space-creating windows the event flag alone would score as failure -- including a side with 73% of the ball that exited on penalties (two non-creating windows). The two layers separate space-creating-but-unconverted from sterile possession, a distinction event-only on-ball value cannot make.

</details>


### [260] [DistMoE: Private-data Rehearsal-free Routing in Mixture-of-Experts for Distributed Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09907)
*Mainak Singha,Niccolò Biondi,Elisa Ricci,Subhankar Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DistMoE是一种面向分布式视觉指令微调的混合专家（MoE）方法，通过给每个客户端配备私有的FFN专家，并在公共锚定专家组合阶段利用各向同性正则化损失更新路由器与轻量级投影适配器，实现无需跨客户端重放的知识融合、模块化路由与灵活的专家复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽有强大的多模态指令跟随能力，但将其适配到多样化的视觉-语言领域通常需要集中式数据访问和昂贵的联合训练。这在数据分散于私有、领域特定或权限受限的客户端时是不现实的，因此需要一种支持分布式、隐私保护的视觉指令调优方法。

Method: DistMoE在每个语言解码器层中，在公共前馈网络（FFN）基础上为每个客户端附加一个客户端特定的私有FFN专家，以获取领域特定知识。为解决独立专家训练导致的表示尺度不一致问题，引入公共锚定专家组合阶段：仅更新路由器与轻量级私有投影适配器，在本地客户端数据与公共数据混合上通过各向同性正则化损失进行训练，实现免跨客户端重放的组合。推理时通过公共与私有专家间的模块化路由实现无需显式领域标签的token级领域组合。

Result: 在多个视觉-语言基准上的实验表明，DistMoE支持灵活的专家复用、有效的领域适配，并在保持对客户端特定知识的模块化控制的同时取得了有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: DistMoE为分布式场景下的多模态指令调优提供了一种可扩展且具备隐私友好性的MoE解决方案，兼顾了领域适配效果与专家知识的模块化可控性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong multimodal instruction-following ability, but adapting them to diverse visual-language domains typically assumes centralized data access and costly joint training. This is restrictive when data is distributed across private, domain-specific, or permission-limited clients. To this end, we propose DistMoE, a mixture-of-experts (MoE) approach for distributed visual instruction tuning. In each layer of the language decoder it augments the public feedforward network (FFN) with a client-specific private FFN expert, with the goal to acquire domain-specific knowledge. However, independent expert training causes the private FFNs to learn representation of different scale and magnitudes, making merging the experts difficult. To reduce client-specific drift, we introduce a public-anchored expert composition stage that updates only routers and lightweight private projection adapters on a mix of local client data and public data, via an isotropic regularization loss, therefore making it cross-client rehearsal-free composition. During inference, DistMoE performs modular routing over public and private experts, enabling token-wise domain composition without explicit domain labels. Experiments across diverse visual-language benchmarks show that DistMoE enables flexible expert reuse, effective domain adaptation, and competitive performance while preserving modular control over client-specific knowledge. Codes are available at https://github.com/mainaksingha01/DistMoE.

</details>


### [261] [Beyond Hazard Resemblance: Contrastive Event Adjudication for Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09908)
*Wenti Yin,Xiang Wang,Huaxin Zhang,Hanqing Wang,Hongbo Shao,Changxin Gao,Nong Sang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CEAVAD 提出一种无需训练的视频异常检测方法，通过将推理单元从孤立异常概念转为可证伪的事件假设，并利用竞争性解释与视频证据之间的交互建立推理时可解释的决策边界，在三个基准上达到训练-free范式下的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有监督视频异常检测需要大量目标域标注数据；现有的训练-free方法虽然借助预训练模型丰富的语义与推理能力，但这些能力本身并不直接构成异常判定准则——更丰富的异常描述能更好地捕捉危害相似性，却无法解决"是否异常"的判定问题。

Method: CEAVAD 包含三个步骤：(1) 利用公共安全知识构建"危害-良性"事件对比，为每个危害机制配对通用的正常解释和机制特异的良性对应解释；(2) 判断目标视频区间是更能支持危害解释还是其良性竞争解释，生成可修订的对比性边界提议；(3) 在竞争性解释之间进行裁决，判断危害假设是否在视频证据下成立，同时支持时间定位异常检测和基于证据的解释输出。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的视频异常检测基准数据集上的实验表明，CEAVAD在训练-free范式下达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: CEAVAD通过将推理对象从单一异常概念转变为可证伪的事件假设，并引入竞争性解释裁决机制，成功地在无需训练的条件下建立了有效的异常决策边界，同时兼具时间定位能力与可解释性。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) aims to identify and temporally localize abnormal events in videos. Supervised methods learn anomaly decision boundaries from target-domain annotations but require substantial in-domain data. Existing training-free methods leverage the rich semantic knowledge and reasoning capabilities of pretrained models to interpret visual content, yet these capabilities do not directly define an anomaly decision criterion: richer anomaly descriptions better capture hazard resemblance without resolving abnormality. To this end, we propose Contrastive Event Adjudication for training-free Video Anomaly Detection (CEAVAD), which shifts the unit of inference from isolated anomaly concepts to falsifiable event hypotheses and establishes an inference-time explanatory boundary through the interaction between competing explanations and video evidence. Specifically, CEAVAD first uses public-safety knowledge to construct hazard-benign event contrasts, pairing each hazard mechanism with a generic normal account and a mechanism-specific benign counterpart. It then determines whether the target interval better supports a hazard explanation or its benign competitor, yielding a revisable contrastive boundary proposal for the target. Finally, CEAVAD adjudicates between the competing explanations to determine whether the hazard hypothesis survives the video evidence, supporting both temporally localized anomaly detection and evidence-grounded explanations. Experiments on three widely used VAD benchmarks demonstrate that CEAVAD achieves state-of-the-art performance under the training-free paradigm.

</details>


### [262] [Learning How the World Evolves: Extrapolative Video World Models via Latent Dynamics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09926)
*Haodong Li,Shaoteng Liu,Tianyu Wang,Chongjian Ge,Sihui Ji,Jiahan Zhang,Xin Lin,Haolin Lu,Zhe Lin,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Dynamics Reasoning (LDR)，该方法将视频扩散模型的隐空间状态转移显式建模为运动学积分，仅回归三阶及以上残差动力学，并在结构化隐空间上运行以实现更好的外推。在PhyWorld白盒物理基准上，LDR在分布外场景中大幅超越视频扩散基线（误差差距缩小20倍以上），参数量少26倍、速度快143倍，并首次实现了视频世界模型对训练分布外动力学的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型主要拟合像素级静态外观，未显式建模像素随时间转移的动力学规律（运动定律），导致生成画面视觉合理但物理上可能不准确，且无法外推到训练分布之外的动态场景。

Method: LDR将隐空间的状态转移建模为显式的运动学积分：低阶动力学项被数值积分，模型仅回归驱动滚动的三阶及以上高阶残差。此外，LDR在结构化隐空间上运行该积分（而非密集卷积特征），从而增强外推能力。验证采用PhyWorld白盒物理基准，涵盖匀速运动、抛物线、碰撞、弹跳和迫近五类任务。

Result: 在256^2分辨率下，无论单任务还是多任务训练，LDR在分布内与分布外误差之间的差距比视频扩散基线小20倍以上，同时参数量仅为基线的1/26，运行速度快143倍。在严重分布偏移下（如仅训练红球水平右移，可正确预测蓝方块水平左移），LDR仍能泛化。

Conclusion: LDR是首个能够将学到的动力学外推到训练分布之外的视频世界模型，证明了显式运动学归纳偏置结合结构化隐空间对学习真实动力学及外推的有效性。
